We show that this can be performed using methods based on gradients in a stochastic optimization framework, provided that care is taken with respect to dominated points. Moreover, in the absence of readily available differentiable code, we propose to use Gaussian Processes as differentiable surrogate models, in order to perform the optimization. An additional contribution in this work are some active learning strategies, through acquisition functions which helps construct a surrogate model well-designed for the multiobjective optimization problem at stake. These strategies are compared on simple analytical problems to assess their performances.

</details>


### [209] [Pretrained Medical Representations for the Practical Screening of Drug Repositioning Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19865)
*Yuhei Fujioka,Daitaro Misawa,Shingo Fukuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一预训练框架，融合层级子词聚合、部分掩码与交叉引用机制，并引入任务自适应表示方法，用于临床事件预测与阿尔茨海默病药物重定位假设的生成与排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于BERT的模型难以充分刻画医疗编码的层级结构以及诊断与治疗之间的复杂交互，导致表示学习虽在疾病预测等任务上有效，却难以扩展到科学假设发现。此外，诊断向量中历史处方信息被过度编码，阻碍假设的稳健排序。

Method: 提出统一预训练框架，显式整合层级子词聚合、部分掩码和交叉引用机制；并在假设排序阶段引入任务自适应表示方法以缓解诊断向量对历史处方信息的过度编码。

Result: 在预训练目标与下游临床事件预测任务（包括痴呆发病与住院）上持续优于现有方法；在针对阿尔茨海默病的计算机药物重定位案例中，无需依赖文献等外部知识即可数据驱动地重新发现已知有前景的药物，并实现假设的稳健排序。

Conclusion: 研究建立了基于观察性关联的假设生成与排序探索性筛选工作流。该框架不提供因果证据，而是为后续严格因果推断筛选候选对象，表明领域知识驱动的表示学习结合任务自适应表示控制可支撑实用的假设发现流程。

Abstract: Representation learning from medical code sequences in electronic health records and medical claims data has been successful in various clinical applications, such as those regarding disease prediction. However, significant challenges remain in extending this approach to the discovery of scientific hypotheses. One reason is that many existing BERT-based models fail to adequately capture the hierarchical structure of medical codes and the complex interactions between diagnoses and treatments. To address these limitations, we propose a new unified pre-training framework that explicitly integrates hierarchical sub-token aggregation, partial masking, and cross-reference mechanisms. The proposed model consistently outperformed existing methods on both pre-training objectives and downstream clinical event prediction tasks, including the onset of dementia and hospitalization. We also conducted an in silico drug repositioning case study targeting Alzheimer's disease. In the hypothesis generation step, our approach successfully rediscovered known promising drugs in a data-driven manner without relying on such external knowledge sources as the literature. Subsequently, in the hypothesis prioritization step, we introduced a Task-Adaptive Representation Approach to alleviate the over-encoding of historical prescription information within diagnostic vectors, enabling the robust prioritization of generated hypotheses. This study establishes an exploratory screening workflow for hypothesis generation and prioritization based on observational associations. Importantly, this framework is not intended to provide causal evidence, but rather to identify promising candidates for subsequent rigorous causal inference. Overall, this study demonstrates that domain-informed representation learning combined with task-adaptive representation control can enable a practical hypothesis discovery workflow.

</details>


### [210] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-Routed Analysis for Email Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19873)
*Omran Berjawi,Walid fahs,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AURA——一种自适应不确定性路由分析的多模态邮件威胁检测系统，结合邮件正文与内嵌 URL，通过不确定性量化实现分级检测，在多个语料上取得高 F1 与良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邮件垃圾与钓鱼攻击仍是关键安全威胁，攻击者借助大语言模型生成语境上更具说服力的恶意邮件，现有检测系统难以跟上节奏；且跨多样、演化攻击场景的泛化能力有限，实际部署后效果下降。

Method: AURA 为多模态检测系统，同时分析邮件正文与嵌入 URL，采用双层架构：第一层由 URL 分类器量化预测不确定性，仅将模糊（不确定）邮件升级至第二层——一个微调后的 Transformer 编码器进行语义分析，从而实现自适应路由。

Result: 在八个异构训练语料与两个跨十年攻击活动的留出真实语料上评估：同分布 macro F1 达 0.9858；在 NazPhish-Eval 与 GuenterTrap-Eval 上分别保持 0.9502 与 0.9436，显示真实分布偏移下的稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: AURA 通过不确定性路由的多模态分级分析，在保持高检测精度的同时显著提升了对演化攻击的泛化性，验证了不确定性驱动路由在邮件威胁检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Email spam and phishing attacks remain a critical security threat. Adversaries increasingly exploit large language models to craft contextually convincing malicious messages, and existing spam detection systems often struggle to keep pace. Generalization across diverse and evolving attack scenarios is limited, which reduces effectiveness once these systems are deployed in practice. This paper introduces Adaptive Uncertainty-Routed Analysis (AURA), a multimodal email threat detection system that analyzes both the content of an email and its embedded URLs. AURA is built around two layers: the first quantifies prediction uncertainty from a URL classifier, and only ambiguous messages are escalated to a fine-tuned transformer encoder for semantic analysis. The system is evaluated on eight heterogeneous training corpora together with two held-out real-world corpora spanning a decade of adversarial campaigns. AURA reaches a macro F1-score of 0.9858 in-distribution, and on NazPhish-Eval and GuenterTrap-Eval it maintains 0.9502 and 0.9436, respectively, which is evidence of robust generalization under genuine distribution shift.

</details>


### [211] [Uni-LaDiR: Latent Diffusion Unifies Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19878)
*Haoqiang Kang,Yizhe Zhang,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yian Ma,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-LaDiR，将多模态推理中的思维token统一到共享潜空间，并用扩散模型预测下一块思维token，在11个VLM基准和2个VLA套件上分别取得7.3%和6.1%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推理方法通常将不同模态的思维token简单拼接为单一序列，模型需自行弥合模态间的表征差异，导致跨模态推理困难。作者希望让来自不同模态的思维共享统一潜空间，从而在推理过程中更自然地进行跨模态信息融合。

Method: Uni-LaDiR包含三部分：1）统一编码器，将不同模态的教师推理步骤映射为共享思维token，并以保留后续推理与最终答案/动作所需信息为目标训练；2）扩散推理器，考虑到同一上下文可支持多种有效后续步骤，采用扩散方式从输入及前序块预测下一块思维token；3）编码器与扩散推理器共享模型权重联合训练，使思维token既对任务有用又可从上下文预测。推理阶段模型无需教师观测即可生成思维token。

Result: 在11个视觉语言模型（VLM）基准和2个视觉语言动作（VLA）套件上，Uni-LaDiR相较最强基线在视觉推理任务上取得7.3%的相对提升，在机器人操作任务上取得6.1%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 将多模态思维统一到共享潜空间并用扩散建模其生成过程，可有效提升跨模态推理与机器人操作性能，验证了潜空间统一思维表示在多模态推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires models to draw on information from multiple modalities throughout the reasoning process. Yet existing methods often concatenate modality-specific thought tokens in a single sequence, leaving the model to bridge representational differences as it reasons across modalities. We introduce Uni-LaDiR (Unified Latent Diffusion Reasoner), a framework that brings these thoughts into a shared latent space for reasoning. A unified encoder maps teacher reasoning steps from different modalities into shared thought tokens, trained to preserve the information needed for later reasoning steps and the final answer or action. Because the same context can support multiple valid next steps, we use diffusion to predict the next block of thought tokens from the input and preceding blocks. Jointly training the encoder and diffusion reasoner with shared model weights encourages thought tokens to be both useful for the task and predictable from the available context. At inference, the model generates these tokens without teacher observations. Across eleven vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks and two vision-language-action (VLA) suites, Uni-LaDiR achieves relative gains over the strongest evaluated baselines of 7.3% on visual reasoning tasks and 6.1% on robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [212] [Online Adaptive Kernel Mixing for Gaussian Process Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19891)
*Kavin Aravindan,Mani Tej Sriram,Gautam Dasarathy,Tejas Bodas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HACK GPs 将核函数选择建模为在线学习专家建议问题，用 AdaHedge 在线更新候选核的权重，并提出 MoG 与分类采样两种变体，在理论上保证权重收敛到最优核，实验上在 BO、LSE、BAL 任务中优于标准核与简单集成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程（GP）广泛用作黑盒函数的代理模型，应用于贝叶斯优化（BO）、水平集估计（LSE）和贝叶斯主动学习（BAL）等序贯决策问题。GP 性能关键取决于核函数，而在核函数错误指定（misspecification）下，标准核可能导致次优决策。因此需要一种能自适应选择核函数的方法。

Method: 提出 HACK GPs（Hedge Adaptive Cumulative Kernels），将核函数选择视为在线学习中的专家建议（learning with expert advice）问题：把每个候选核视为一个 GP“专家”，基于作为拟合函数能力与任务目标对齐程度的代理损失，使用 AdaHedge 在线更新专家上的分布。方法提供两种变体：(i) 高斯混合（MoG），(ii) 分类采样（categorical sampling）。

Result: 理论方面，在损失间隔（loss-gap）条件下建立了通用保证，证明权重会集中到最优核上，且由此得到的采集函数接近最优专家的采集函数。实验方面，在 BO、LSE 和 BAL 任务上，相较于平方指数核（Squared Exponential）、Matern-5/2 等标准核以及简单集成基线，观察到稳健的性能表现。

Conclusion: HACK GPs 通过在线专家建议框架实现核函数的自适应选择，兼具理论保证与跨任务的稳健实证表现，为 GP 代理模型在核错误指定下的序贯决策提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Gaussian Processes (GPs) are widely used as surrogates for black-box functions in sequential decision-making problems such as Bayesian optimization (BO), level set estimation (LSE), and Bayesian active learning (BAL). GP performance critically depends on kernels, and standard kernels can lead to suboptimal decisions under misspecification. To address this, we introduce HACK GPs (Hedge Adaptive Cumulative Kernels), a method that views kernel selection as an online learning with expert advice problem. HACK treats each candidate kernel as a GP "expert" and updates a distribution over experts online using AdaHedge, based on a loss received as a proxy for their ability to fit the function and align with the task objective. We provide two variants of HACK: (i) Mixture of Gaussians (MoG) and (ii) categorical sampling. We establish general guarantees showing that, under a loss-gap condition, the weight concentrates on the best kernel and the resulting acquisition function is close to that of the best expert. Empirically, we observe robust performance across BO, LSE, and BAL compared to standard kernels such as Squared Exponential and Matern-5/2, as well as simple ensemble baselines.

</details>


### [213] [REARL: A Closed-loop Autonomous Driving Simulation Enhancement Framework with Real Traffic Data and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19903)
*Xiaojun Bi,Jun Jiang,Yiwen Sun,Quanyi Ou,Ke Cheng,Mingjie Bi,Yexin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REARL是一个闭环仿真增强框架，将真实交通数据与LLM结合，通过聚类代表性场景、滑动窗口检测偏移并由LLM调整车辆决策，从而缩小仿真分布与真实交通的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶开发依赖高保真仿真，但现有仿真器基于预定义规则或静态数据回放，难以刻画动态交通；CRITICAL虽用真实数据与LLM调整初始配置，但随rollout推进仿真分布仍偏离真实交通。

Method: 对真实交通数据聚类，以各聚类中心作为代表性场景，为LLM提供典型真实交通模式；采用定时滑动窗口检测器监控车辆速度分布与车辆对平均间距的偏差；当指标超过阈值时由LLM调整车辆决策，否则保留原控制器；LLM还从交通快照中选择匹配的真实车辆，并参照该真实动作调制仿真车辆。

Result: 在受控HighD高速公路场景下，与CRITICAL基线及基于PPO的学习基线相比，REARL将速度分布的Hellinger距离降至0.3067，平均间距的MAPE降至0.8371，同时实现22.8575的时间车头间距（THW）和0.0708的换道率。

Conclusion: REARL通过真实数据聚类、在线偏差检测与LLM闭环决策调制，有效提升了仿真交通与真实交通分布的拟合度，在多项指标上优于现有基线。

Abstract: Accurate simulation is crucial for autonomous driving development, yet capturing real-world traffic complexity remains challenging. Existing simulators that rely on predefined rules or static data playback struggle with dynamic traffic. CRITICAL uses real traffic data and a large language model (LLM) to adjust the initial simulation configuration, but the simulated distribution still diverges from real traffic as the rollout evolves. We propose REARL, a closed-loop simulation enhancement framework that integrates real traffic data with LLMs. Real traffic data are clustered, and each cluster center is used as a representative scenario that provides typical real-world traffic patterns for the LLM. A timed sliding-window detector then monitors discrepancies in vehicle speed distribution and mean spacing between pairs of vehicles. If a metric exceeds a threshold, the LLM adjusts vehicle decision-making; otherwise the existing controller is kept. The LLM also selects a matching real vehicle from a traffic snapshot and modulates the simulated vehicle with reference to that real action. In a controlled HighD highway setting, compared with the CRITICAL baseline and a PPO-based learning baseline, REARL reduces the Hellinger distance for speed distributions to 0.3067 and the MAPE for mean spacing to 0.8371, while achieving a time headway (THW) of 22.8575 and a lane change rate of 0.0708.

</details>


### [214] [Digital Twins for Opinion Dynamics: A Generative LLM Framework for Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19913)
*Omran Berjawi,Giuseppe Fenza,Rida Khatoun,Sherali Zeadally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于"数字孪生"的舆论动力学仿真框架：克隆真实Twitter网络，为智能体赋予人格、情绪、中心性、固执度、影响力等属性，用Mistral-7B依据记忆与社会暴露进行观点更新；在COVID-19与2020美国大选两个真实数据集上验证，个体预测误差较最优经典基线降低50%以上，并改善结构对齐与极化动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交网络中的舆论动力学是计算社会科学的核心问题，与政治极化、虚假信息及公共卫生响应直接相关。现有方法存在两类局限：一是简化的数学模型忽略了与信念更新相关的语言与语境因素；二是基于大语言模型（LLM）的仿真缺乏对真实数据的验证，保真度不明。

Method: 构建"数字孪生"式仿真框架：克隆真实Twitter交互网络作为底层结构；为每个智能体分配persona、情绪、中心性、固执度、影响力等属性；采用Mistral-7B，基于智能体的记忆与社会暴露（social exposure）执行观点更新。以COVID-19话语与2020美国大选两个真实Twitter数据集进行外部验证，并与经典基线对比，同时开展消融实验检验各组件贡献。

Result: 预测精度显著提升：Mistral-7B在COVID-19与2020美国大选数据集上的MAE分别为0.150与0.121，个体预测误差较表现最优的经典基线降低逾50%；结构对齐（Delta_r = 0.120、0.180）与极化动态（Delta_Var = 0.106、0.115）亦有类似改善。消融实验表明，智能体属性、记忆与社会暴露均对观点轨迹的预测保真度有贡献，其中智能体属性贡献最为关键。

Conclusion: 将Mistral-7B以经验克隆的交互网络为基底，可构建出能够复现复杂社会动态的真实感仿真框架。该工作打通了LLM仿真与真实数据验证之间的缺口，为舆论动力学研究提供了可验证的数字孪生范式。

Abstract: The study of opinion dynamics in social networks is one of the key challenges in computational social science with direct relevance to understanding political polarization, misinformation, and health responses. Current approaches focus on simplified mathematical models that ignore linguistic and contextual factors related to belief updates or use Large Language Model (LLM)-based simulations that have not been validated against real data. We present a framework based on the concept of a digital twin to simulate opinion dynamics in social networks. The approach fills the gap by cloning a real-world Twitter network, assigns a set of attributes for agents (such as persona, emotions, centrality, stubbornness, and influence), and employs Mistral-7B to perform opinion update based on memory and social exposure. To evaluate the proposed approach, we validate it against two real Twitter datasets (COVID-19 discourse and U.S elections 2020). The results show that the capability of the proposed framework reproduces opinion trajectories and reduces individual prediction error by more than 50% compared to the best-performing classical baseline (Mistral-7B achieves Mean Absolute Error (MAE) = 0.150 and 0.121 on the COVID-19 and US Election 2020 datasets, respectively). We observe similar improvements in structural alignment (Delta_r = 0.120 and 0.180) and polarization dynamics (Delta_Var = 0.106 and 0.115) on the two datasets, respectively. Additionally, the ablation studies confirm that agent attributes, memory, and social exposure all contribute to the framework's predictive fidelity in reproducing opinion trajectories, with agent attributes being the most critical contributor. Overall, our results demonstrate that grounding Mistral-7B within empirically cloned interaction networks produces a realistic simulation framework capable of reproducing complex social dynamics.

</details>


### [215] [Amortizing Physics-Informed Neural Solvers via Graph Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19915)
*Cheng Jing,Abhishek Verma,Kallol Bera,Yixuan He,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用算子图（operator graph）显式刻画PDE中字段、导数、项与残差的关系，并通过图超网络生成对角编码来初始化元训练的可分解PINN，在无解标签条件下实现跨方程适配；实验表明在双向耦合问题上图描述显著优于项集与系数向量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摊销式（amortized）PINN求解器需要将每个PDE方程描述给可复用求解器。传统系数向量仅在预定义槽位中编码数值参数，使算子与跨字段的赋值关系保持隐式，难以表达方程结构，限制了跨方程泛化。

Method: 将方程关系显式建模为算子图：节点表示字段、导数、项与残差，系数作为项属性保留。由图超网络（graph hypernetwork）生成对角编码，用于初始化经元训练的可分解PINN（factorized PINN）。元训练与目标适配均仅使用控制方程与给定条件，不使用解标签。在固定适配预算下比较系数向量、基于DeepSets的项集、图条件三种描述方式。

Result: 标量对流-扩散-反应问题中，两种基于项的描述均改善高反应速率下的精度且性能相近；双场Fisher-KPP中，元训练仅见非耦合与单向耦合系统，在未见过的双向耦合上适配3000步后，图描述的平均最终误差比项集低35.7%、比系数向量低67.7%；在固定结构电容耦合等离子体模型中，系数向量表现最佳。

Conclusion: 结果支持在系数条件化基础上引入显式方程关系，以提升物理求解器的适配能力；但优势依赖问题结构（如耦合复杂度），固定结构场景下简单系数向量仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Amortizing physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) across related PDEs requires describing each equation to a reusable solver. Coefficient vectors encode numerical parameters in predefined slots, leaving operator and cross-field assignments implicit. We make these relationships explicit in an operator graph, with nodes for fields, derivatives, terms, and residuals and coefficients retained as term attributes. A graph hypernetwork generates diagonal codes that initialize a meta-trained factorized PINN for each target equation. Meta-training and target-specific adaptation use governing equations and prescribed conditions without solution labels. We compare coefficient-vector, DeepSets-based term-set, and graph conditioning by solution accuracy within a fixed adaptation budget. In scalar convection-diffusion-reaction problems, both term-based descriptors improve high-reaction accuracy, with similar performance. In two-field Fisher-KPP, meta-training sees uncoupled and one-way systems; after 3,000 adaptation steps on unseen two-way coupling, the graph's mean final error is 35.7% below the term set and 67.7% below the coefficient vector. In a fixed-structure capacitively coupled plasma model, the coefficient vector performs best. These results support extending coefficient conditioning with explicit equation relationships for physics-based solver adaptation.

</details>


### [216] [One Intervention per Component is Enough: Towards Identifiability in Linear Stochastic Dynamics from Steady State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19955)
*Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从稳态观测与干预数据中恢复多元 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) 过程参数的问题，在仅有稳态"快照"测量、无时间序列轨迹的情形下建立了可辨识性结果并提出递归学习算法与正则化最小二乘估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用（如大规模基因扰动实验）只能获得稳态"快照"测量，无法获取时间序列轨迹，导致依赖轨迹的经典随机微分方程估计方法失效，因此需要研究仅基于稳态数据（含干预）的 OU 过程参数恢复问题。

Method: 1) 建立可辨识性理论：证明对漂移图的每个强连通分量 (SCC) 施加一次干预，即可在全局尺度因子意义下泛型恢复全部 OU 参数，前提是 SCC 凝聚图连通且具有单一根节点，并满足一定谱非退化假设。2) 提出递归学习算法：对 SCC 进行拓扑排序，逐个分量分离其边缘动力学，结合上游分量已恢复的参数，求解由稳态矩方程导出的线性系统。3) 提出正则化最小二乘估计器：联合最小化观测与干预数据下稳态均值方程和协方差方程的残差。

Result: 实验验证了所提理论结论，能够有效恢复底层 OU 过程的参数。

Conclusion: 每个 SCC 一次干预即可泛型辨识 OU 参数（至全局尺度因子），结合拓扑排序的递归算法与联合稳态矩残差的正则化最小二乘估计器，可在仅使用稳态观测与干预数据的情况下实现参数恢复。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the parameters of a multivariate Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) process from steady-state observational and interventional data. In many applications, such as large-scale gene perturbation experiments, only stationary "snapshot" measurements are available, making standard stochastic differential equation estimation methods that rely on time-series trajectories inapplicable. We first establish an identifiability result: one intervention per strongly connected component (SCC) of the drift graph suffices to recover all OU process parameters generically up to a global scaling factor. This holds provided that the SCC condensation graph is connected with a single root and certain spectral nondegeneracy assumptions hold. We propose a recursive learning algorithm that orders SCCs topologically and, for each component, isolates its marginal dynamics and solves a linear system derived from the steady-state moment equations, leveraging parameters recovered for upstream components. Building on this theoretical foundation, we propose a regularized least-squares estimator that jointly minimizes residuals of the steady-state mean and covariance equations across observational and interventional data. Experimental results validate our theoretical findings in recovering parameters of the underlying OU process.

</details>


### [217] [Graph-Based Stochastic Power-UCT: Monte-Carlo Graph Search with Power Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19956)
*Tung Tran,Viet Bao Mai,Hoang Ta,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图结构随机Power-UCT算法GS-Power-UCT及其全状态变体，通过跨轨迹共享同深度状态提升随机MDP规划样本效率，并给出收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树式MCTS在随机MDP中，同一状态经不同轨迹到达时被重复创建，导致模拟样本浪费。核心动机是复用共享状态样本以提升规划效率，同时不牺牲收敛性。

Method: GS-Power-UCT：按规划深度共享状态（同一深度合并，不同深度分开存储值），适用于含环的一般随机MDP。另设两种全状态变体：GS-Power-UCT-F，每个物理状态仅存一个节点以增强样本共享；GS-Power-UCT-F+，引入自适应视界控制跨深度值混合偏差。

Result: 证明固定规划视界下根节点估计以O(n^{-1/2})收敛到有限视界值，与树式Stochastic-Power-UCT同阶，同时跨共享状态复用样本；GS-Power-UCT-F+在跨深度间隙消失时收敛到根状态的最优无限视界折扣值V*(s0)。随机规划基准实验显示样本效率优于树式和图式基线。

Conclusion: 深度感知的图结构状态共享可在保持树式MCTS理论收敛速率的同时显著提高样本效率，全状态变体配合自适应视界可扩展到无限视界最优值估计。

Abstract: Tree-based Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) duplicates the same state when it is reached through different trajectories, which can waste simulations in stochastic MDPs. We introduce Graph-Based Stochastic-Power-UCT (GS-Power-UCT), which shares states reached at the same planning depth while keeping separate values for states reached at different depths. This design applies to general stochastic MDPs, including problems with cycles. We prove that for a fixed planning horizon, the root estimate converges to the finite-horizon value at rate $O(n^{-1/2})$, matching tree-based Stochastic-Power-UCT while reusing samples across shared states. We also study two full-state variants: GS-Power-UCT-F, which stores one node per physical state to increase sample sharing but may mix values from different remaining horizons, and GS-Power-UCT-F$^+$, which uses an adaptive horizon to control this bias. The latter converges to $V^{\star}(s_0)$, the optimal infinite-horizon discounted value at the root state $s_0$, when the remaining cross-depth gap vanishes. Experiments on stochastic planning benchmarks show improved sample efficiency over tree-based and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [218] [CellRFT: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Single-Cell Perturbation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19970)
*Jie Yan,Li Liu,Hanze Guo,Jiaxin Hu,Houxin He,Xiaoning Qi,Haoran Wang,Cong Li,Zhong-Yuan Zhang,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellRFT：以生物评价指标作为直接训练反馈的强化微调框架，通过策略梯度与分层奖励聚合，提升单细胞扰动预测的生物学表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动模型多优化代理损失，与评估所用的生物学标准不一致，导致数据拟合更好未必带来更好的生物学预测，存在目标错配问题。

Method: 提出CellRFT强化微调框架：将生物学评估作为直接训练反馈，采用策略梯度优化从生成细胞群体的不可微评价中学习，并通过分层奖励聚合整合多种生物学奖励。

Result: 实验表明CellRFT可适用于不同预训练模型，有效提升扰动预测；发现优化某一生物学准则可能促进或损害其他准则；互补奖励可改善未被直接优化的指标。

Conclusion: CellRFT提供了探究生物学指标如何塑造模型行为的途径，并可为评估指标设计提供参考，代码将公开。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to perturbations supports the study of gene function, disease mechanisms, and therapeutic strategies. Despite advances in single-cell perturbation modeling, existing models typically optimize surrogate losses that do not directly reflect the biological criteria used for evaluation, so better data fitting need not yield better biological predictions. To address this mismatch, we introduce \textbf{CellRFT}, a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that uses biological evaluation as direct training feedback. CellRFT uses policy-gradient optimization to learn from non-differentiable evaluations of generated cell populations and integrates multiple biological rewards through hierarchical reward aggregation. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate CellRFT's applicability across different pretrained models and effectiveness in improving perturbation prediction, reveal that optimizing one biological criterion can help or hinder others, and show that complementary rewards can improve criteria beyond those directly optimized, offering a way to probe how biological metrics shape model behavior, with the potential to inform evaluation design. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [219] [Past, Future, All at Once: Mitigating Stability-Plasticity Dilemma via Post-hoc JANUS Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19985)
*Zhilong Zheng,Letian Tao,Yang Guan,Yujie Yang,Wei Xiong,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Jingliang Duan,Keqiang Li,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JANUS框架，通过将参数更新投影到Jacobian零空间实现参数空间正交性，从而在微调中恢复被遗忘的历史知识，兼顾稳定性与可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基础模型微调中普遍存在灾难性遗忘问题。现有基于参数高效微调的方法多依赖过于严格的正交子空间条件，难以充分保留历史性能，且往往与微调过程耦合。

Method: 提出纯事后（post-hoc）且与微调方式无关的权重校正框架JANUS，将参数更新投影到Jacobian零空间（JANUS），实现一阶意义上保留历史性能的充分必要条件（参数空间正交性）。为克服Jacobian近似仅局部有效的问题，引入多步自适应校正机制，利用JANUS位移动态验证有效信赖域并调整步长；并结合ghost投影、ghost方向比较与序列级奇异值分解压缩技术提升时空效率。

Result: 实验表明JANUS可与多种微调方法无缝集成，在不干扰下游任务适配的前提下显著恢复历史知识，有效缓解稳定性-可塑性困境。

Conclusion: JANUS以事后校正方式实现参数空间正交性，兼顾历史知识保留与下游适配，并提供高效实现技术，为缓解灾难性遗忘提供了通用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning foundation models on new tasks inevitably suffer from catastrophic forgetting. While existing works attempt to mitigate this on the basis of parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, they adopted an overly restrictive Subspace Orthogonality condition. In this paper, we introduce a purely post-hoc and tuning-agnostic weight rectification framework that achieves Parameter Space Orthogonality, which is the necessary and sufficient condition for preserving historical performance to the first order. By projecting parameter updates into the JAcobian NUll Space (JANUS), our method significantly recovers compromised historical knowledge without interfering with the underlying fine-tuning process. To overcome the local validity of the Jacobian approximation, we further propose a Multi-step Adaptive Rectification mechanism that utilizes the JANUS shift to dynamically verify the valid trust region and adjust step sizes. Coupled with our proposed ghost projection, ghost orientation comparison, and sequence-level singular value decomposition compression techniques, JANUS also achieves great temporal and spatial efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that JANUS seamlessly integrates with various fine-tuning methods, significantly mitigating the stability-plasticity dilemma by recovering historical knowledge while preserving downstream task adaptation.

</details>


### [220] [EPIG-Tree: Compute-Optimal Branching for Gradient-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20004)
*Nikita Khomich,Leopold Hermansson,Ido Hakimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将树状 rollout 构建视为策略梯度估计的计算分配问题，提出 EPIG-Tree，按“每单位计算对策略梯度不确定性的削减”来放置分支，在连续控制、冻结 LLM 校准与在线多轮任务上均优于 GRPO 及熵分支基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励的强化学习（如 GRPO）把整条随机轨迹压缩为单一标量奖励，简洁可扩展，但探索与奖励分配效率低：轨迹中大量因果决策、恢复尝试与环境随机事件共享同一个轨迹级优势。因此需要研究如何更高效地分配 rollout 计算以估计策略梯度。

Method: 将树状 rollout 构建建模为策略梯度估计的计算分配问题，主张分支应放在“增加一个分支能最大程度降低单位计算下策略梯度不确定性”的位置。基于局部策略梯度随机变量的全方差分解，导出两条分配律：新分支降低决策不确定性，重复后缀 rollout 降低延续不确定性。据此提出 EPIG-Tree 分数，利用已计算 rollout，估计占用与分数加权的价值不确定性，并给出后缀律 n_e ∝ w_e ‖∇_θ log π(a_e|h_e)‖ σ_e / √c_e。

Result: 在克隆状态控制中，EPIG 降低梯度 MSE，在 13 环境扫描的 9 个密集连续控制环境全部胜出，并近乎完美恢复参考梯度方向；相对熵分支改善了冻结 LLM 的梯度校准。在线单轮数学任务中，树局部信用优于扁平 GRPO，但分支放置的作用次于 token 级信用分配。在线多轮 Wordle 中，EPIG 取得最高最终胜率 0.850，超过早期饱和于 0.790 的扁平 GRPO 以及熵分支，且随训练推进优势扩大。

Conclusion: 按梯度不确定性增益分配分支的树状 rollout 能更高效地估计策略梯度，这一优势可迁移到有状态、大动作空间的场景；在分支放置之外，token 级信用分配同样起关键作用，二者共同决定最终性能。

Abstract: Reward-based reinforcement learning for language models, exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), collapses an entire stochastic trajectory into a single scalar reward. This is clean and scalable, but it explores and allocates reward inefficiently: a trajectory may contain many causal decisions, recovery attempts, and environment-randomness events, yet every token or action inherits one trajectory-level advantage. We study tree-based rollout construction as a compute-allocation problem for policy-gradient estimation. Our central claim is that branches should be placed not where the policy is merely uncertain, but where an additional branch most reduces uncertainty about the policy gradient per unit of compute. From a law-of-total-variance decomposition of the local policy-gradient random variable, we derive two allocation laws: new branches reduce decision uncertainty, while repeated suffix rollouts reduce continuation uncertainty. The resulting EPIG-Tree score allocates branches using the already computed rollouts. It estimates occupancy- and score-weighted value uncertainty, along with a suffix law $n_e \propto w_e \|\nabla_θ\log π(a_e|h_e)\| σ_e / \sqrt{c_e}$. Empirically, EPIG reduces gradient MSE in cloned-state control, winning in all nine dense continuous-control environments of a 13-environment sweep and recovering the reference gradient direction near-perfectly, and it improves frozen-LLM gradient calibration relative to entropy branching. In online single-turn math, tree-local credit beats flat GRPO, while branch placement is secondary to token-level credit assignment. In online multi-turn Wordle, EPIG attains the highest final win rate (0.850), overtaking flat GRPO, which saturates early at 0.790, and entropy branching as training proceeds, confirming that the gradient-estimation advantage transfers to a stateful, large-action setting.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic Generalized Gromov-Wasserstein Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20008)
*Junda Ying,Zhiwei Zeng,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TP-DATE框架，将Gromov–Wasserstein最优传输动态化，以无模拟方式重建连续轨迹，并证明静态-动态等价性，在空间转录组数据上提升结构保持与三维动态重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组的动态重建既需匹配表达模式，又需保持组织空间结构。现有Gromov–Wasserstein最优传输（GW-OT）多为静态形式，可用于结构感知对齐，但缺少重建连续轨迹的一般动态表述。

Method: 提出Travelling Pair Dynamical Alignment and Trajectory Estimation（TP-DATE）。通过路径作用量刻画一大类静态与动态二次型最优传输（QOT），并证明静态-动态等价；进一步提出travelling-pair flow matching，使条件路径可相互作用，并将其交互边缘化为单一向量场，实现无模拟训练。

Result: 在合成数据和真实空间转录组数据上，TP-DATE更好地保持空间结构，并改善连续三维动态重建效果。

Conclusion: TP-DATE为GW-OT提供了通用的动态推广，兼具理论等价性与无模拟计算优势，适用于结构感知的连续轨迹重建，特别是在空间转录组场景中。

Abstract: Gromov--Wasserstein optimal transport (GW-OT) extends classical optimal transport by introducing structure-aware transport cost. This is particularly relevant for spatial transcriptomics, where dynamical reconstruction should preserve tissue structure in addition to matching expression patterns. While static formulations have been widely used for such structure-aware alignment, a general dynamic formulation for reconstructing continuous trajectories is still missing. We introduce Travelling Pair Dynamical Alignment and Trajectory Estimation (TP-DATE), a theoretical and computational framework to generalize GW-OT dynamically in a simulation-free manner. We formulate a broad class of static and dynamic Quadratic-form OT (QOT) through path actions and prove the static dynamic equivalence. We further develop travelling-pair flow matching, which allows interacting conditional paths and marginalizes their interactions into a single vector field. On synthetic and real spatial transcriptomics data, TP-DATE better preserves spatial structure and improves continuous 3D dynamics reconstruction.

</details>


### [222] [Correct Now, Insufficient Later: Auditing Update Sufficiency in Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20045)
*Guangzhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"配对历史审计"方法来检测记忆系统在共享未来更新后是否需要保留当前的区分信息，并通过多机制多条件的合成实验进行故障分析与修复验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统可能正确回答当前查询，却丢弃了后续更新所需的区分信息，这种失败模式难以通过单点问答评估发现。

Method: 配对历史审计：构造两条历史，当前答案相同、共享同一未来更新、但需给出不同的后续答案；在6种合成机制、12种记忆条件、2次重复、2个模型后端上评估24对历史，并进行记录级审计与标签等变修复。

Result: 确定性前沿选择器严格揭示准确率为DeepSeek 96/96、GLM 82/96；结构化写入器为62次成功（1个未决）与56/96；四结果联合对比的有限样本识别区间为[0.521,0.542]和[0.292,0.313]；发现26与25个格式正确但语义错误的结构化揭示记忆，14个GLM失败均为"正确值放入错误包装"；墓碑移除导致目标机制16/16精确重放失败；标识符重命名使原始前沿晚期引用充分性从8/8降至94/320；修复仅保留2/8原始晚期引用答案。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种有界的评估方法与可复现的故障分析，而非证明修复算法的普遍优越性；未声称独立留出集或自然任务验证。

Abstract: A memory can answer a current query correctly while discarding distinctions required by a later update. We investigate this failure with a paired-history audit: two histories have the same current answer, receive a shared future update, and require different subsequent answers. A pilot evaluates 24 history pairs across six synthetic mechanisms, 12 memory conditions, two repeats, and two model backends. A deterministic frontier selector obtains strict reveal accuracy of 96/96 on DeepSeek and 82/96 on GLM; a structured writer obtains 62 successes with one unresolved outcome and 56/96. The configured four-outcome joint contrast has finite-sample identification intervals of [0.521, 0.542] and [0.292, 0.313], not confidence intervals. A record-level audit distinguishes retained-state adequacy, response delivery, and answer-schema compliance without changing those original scores. It finds 26 and 25 well-formed but semantically wrong structured reveal memories, while all 14 GLM frontier reveal failures contain correct values in the wrong wrapper. Tombstone removal produces 16/16 exact replay failures in the targeted mechanism. Identifier renaming then exposes a separate flaw: original frontier late-reference adequacy falls from 8/8 to 94/320 transformed instances. We provide and test a label-equivariant repair, but it preserves only 2/8 original late-reference answers: eliminating a naming shortcut does not solve unknown future relevance. These results support a scoped evaluation methodology and reproducible failure analysis, not general superiority of the repaired algorithm. Paid pilot evidence, retrospective diagnostics, and new offline tests are reported separately; no independent held-out or natural-task validation is claimed.

</details>


### [223] [Evaluating Explanation Methods by the Predictors They Induce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20058)
*Jacob Selbæk,Hugo L. Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的解释质量检验方法：将解释转化为预测器（读取各特征效应并相加），在未见数据上评估其还原模型预测的能力；该检验无需拟合，适用于PDP、ALE、SHAP、LIME等任何可写成特征函数的解释。理论证明特征独立时求和部分依赖曲线给出最优加性摘要，而特征相关时失效。在13个真实数据集、9个合成设计、4类模型家族上，最优方法完全取决于特征相关性：独立时SHAP略逊于PDP（与理论一致），相关真实数据上SHAP领先；部分常用质量指标甚至偏好被破坏的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释评估标准往往难以比较、缺乏统一基准，作者希望用"能否从解释重建模型预测"这一更简单、更客观的检验来评判解释质量。

Method: 将每个解释转化为预测器：读取每个特征效应并相加，在未见数据上测量其对模型预测的还原程度；不做任何拟合，得分仅反映解释本身。方法适用于任何可写为特征函数的解释，并在PDP、ALE、SHAP、LIME上演示。

Result: 理论上证明特征独立时求和部分依赖曲线是最优加性摘要，特征相关时该结论失效。实验中，13个真实数据集、9个合成设计、4类模型家族上最优方法取决于特征相关性：独立时SHAP略差于PDP（符合理论），相关真实数据上SHAP领先；部分常用质量指标甚至偏好受损解释而非完好解释。

Conclusion: 解释方法的优劣并非绝对，而是由特征相关性决定；基于重建的检验提供了一种简单、可比较且反映解释本质的评估方式，并揭示现有质量指标的缺陷。

Abstract: Explanations of machine learning models are usually judged by criteria that are hard to compare. We propose a simpler test: if an explanation really describes how a model uses its features, it should be possible to rebuild the model's predictions from it. We turn each explanation into a predictor by reading each feature's effect and adding them up, and measure how well that predictor reproduces the model on unseen data. Nothing is fitted, so the score reflects the explanation itself. The test applies to any explanation that can be written as a function of the features; we demonstrate it on partial dependence plots (PDP), accumulated local effects (ALE), SHAP and LIME. We prove that summing partial dependence curves gives the best possible additive summary of a model when its features are independent, and that this fails when they are dependent. Across 13 real datasets and 9 synthetic designs and four model families, which method scores best depends entirely on feature dependence: where features are independent SHAP is slightly worse than PDP, exactly as the theory predicts; on dependent real data SHAP leads. Some widely used quality metrics even prefer a damaged explanation to an intact one.

</details>


### [224] [MATCH: Model-Aware Tool Learning with Curriculum Scheduling and Hierarchically Gated Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20082)
*Shihao Liu,Hao Yin,Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MATCH框架，通过模型感知的课程学习与分层门控奖励，解决工具学习中的课程错位与奖励泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的工具调用优化存在两个问题：固定阈值课程与策略演化中的能力边界不匹配；加性奖励在工具预测错误时会产生参数级信用泄漏。

Method: MATCH包含两部分：模型感知课程学习（MACL）维护与策略共同演化的样本难度，每轮选取能力边界附近样本及top-k困难样本池；分层工具调用门控奖励（HTGR）将工具名、参数键、参数值作为门控链评分，仅在前置条件满足时给予信用。HTGR奖励同时驱动GRPO更新与MACL难度刷新，形成闭环。

Result: 在API-Bank和BFCL V3上整体准确率分别达72.19%和62.87%，优于主要监督与RL基线；跨两个模型家族四种骨干网络均有一致提升。

Conclusion: MATCH通过课程调度与分层门控奖励的闭环设计，有效提升了LLM工具调用行为的学习效率与准确率，并具备跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Tool learning enables large language models (LLMs) to use external tools for tasks beyond parametric knowledge. Reinforcement learning can optimize tool-call behavior from feedback, but current methods still face two problems: fixed-threshold curricula can become misaligned with the policy's evolving capability boundary, and additive rewards can leak argument-level credit when the predicted tool is wrong. To address these problems, we propose MATCH, a closed-loop framework for model-aware tool learning with curriculum scheduling and hierarchically gated rewards. Model-Aware Curriculum Learning (MACL) maintains reward-derived sample difficulty that co-evolves with the policy, and each epoch selects samples near the current capability boundary together with a top-k pool of harder cases. Hierarchical Tool-call Gated Reward (HTGR) scores tool name, argument key, and argument value as a gated chain, granting credit at each level only when prerequisites hold. The same HTGR rewards drive both GRPO updates and MACL's difficulty refresh, closing the loop between policy optimization and sample scheduling. On API-Bank and BFCL V3, MATCH reaches 72.19% and 62.87% overall accuracy, outperforming the main supervised and RL-based baselines. Backbone experiments further show consistent improvements across four backbones from two model families.

</details>


### [225] [SETTer: Sparse-Encoder Transformer for Long-term Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20086)
*Abraham Ezema,Chijioke Eze,Ferdinanda Ponci,Antonello Monti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETTer：一种基于Transformer的长时多元时间序列预测模型，通过解耦自注意力与混合掩码技术缓解过平滑、容量受限和不可解释性问题，在88%的实验场景中优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时多元时间序列广泛存在于电力系统、交易等领域，但因其高维度和复杂关系，传统预测方法难以准确建模。Transformer类方法凭借注意力机制在长时预测中表现优异，但在复杂高维输入下存在过平滑、模型容量受限和可解释性差（不透明）的问题。

Method: 提出SETTer模型：1) 引入解耦自注意力（decoupled self-attention）；2) 采用混合掩码（hybrid masking）技术，使模型能在时间维度与通道维度上有效捕捉主导的短期与长期模式；3) 在模型层中加入简单的可解释结构，以指示其判别性模式；4) 使用单层Transformer架构建模长时依赖。

Result: 在真实世界长时多元时间序列预测基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SETTer在88%的场景中优于当前最先进的模型；单层Transformer架构即可在数据复杂度各异的情况下有效建模长期依赖关系。

Conclusion: SETTer通过解耦自注意力与混合掩码，在保持单层Transformer轻量结构的同时，兼顾了对时空/通道模式的有效建模与可解释性，验证了其在长时多元时间序列预测上的优越性能。

Abstract: Long-term multivariate time series plays a significant role in many application areas such as power systems, trading, etc. However, their accurate prediction is quite difficult for conventional forecasting methods as they often exhibit high dimensionality and complex relationships. Recent works show that transformer-based approaches are quite effective for long-term forecasting thanks to their attention mechanism. However, in the presence of complex high-dimensional inputs, they show evidence of oversmoothing, limited capacity, and opacity. To this end, this paper introduces SETTer, a transformer-based model that addresses these challenges by incorporating novel techniques for decoupled self-attention and hybrid masking. The proposed techniques enable SETTer to effectively capture the dominant short- and long-term patterns across the temporal and channel dimensions. In addition, we enrich the model layers with simple explainable structures that indicate the discriminative pattern of SETTer. We show that with a single-layer transformer architecture, SETTer can effectively model long-term dependencies in the presence of varying data complexities. Extensive experiments on real-word benchmark datasets for long-term multivariate time series forecasting demonstrate that SETTer outperforms state-of-the-art models in 88% of the scenarios.

</details>


### [226] [CARE-VI: Conservative Adaptive Reliability Estimation for Value Improvement in Off-Policy Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20098)
*Xiang Zou,Shengzhu Shi,Junqi Gao,Zhichang Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CARE-VI框架，通过CARS候选排序筛选、SEVA选择器-评估器价值评估和DARE动态风险感知增强三部分，构建可靠的自适应时序差分目标，在十二个MuJoCo设置中取得最高平均回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略演员-评论家学习中，直接价值改进虽能细化下一状态目标，但候选动作的排序、审查与加权存在风险：噪声排序导致过早承诺候选，复用选择分数使目标估值有偏，固定增强权重放大弱证据，影响目标可靠性。

Method: CARE-VI由三部分组成：CARS在预设预算内保留有序候选前缀，仅当观测边界间隙超过分歧缩放的不确定性半径时收缩；SEVA用选择器评论家排序候选、独立参数化评估器评论家审查所选价值，并将审查值上限设为选择器参考值；DARE依据候选可靠性、选择器与评估器信号差距及有限阶段因子调节残差修正。

Result: 理论上给出CARS边界误差、SEVA选择值高估及DARE残差位移相对总体对应量单边偏差的界，并在有限阶段扰动结束后建立固定策略恢复性。实验在四个MuJoCo任务上用SAC、TD3、TD7验证，CARE-VI在全部十二个设置中平均回报最高；分组消融与标量诊断支持三组件对提升目标可靠性的作用。

Conclusion: CARE-VI作为证据调节的目标构建框架，在保持评论家回归与演员更新接口不变的前提下，通过排序筛选、双重评论家评估与动态风险感知增强，有效提升离策略演员-评论家学习的目标可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable temporal-difference targets are central to off-policy actor-critic learning. Direct value improvement refines the next-state target with alternative actions, but the reliability of this refinement depends on how candidate actions are ranked, reviewed, and weighted. Noisy rankings may force premature candidate commitment, reusing selection scores may bias target valuation, and fixed enhancement weights may amplify weak evidence. To address these risks, we develop Conservative Adaptive Ranking and Screening (CARS), which retains an ordered candidate prefix within a preset budget and narrows it only when the observed boundary gap exceeds a disagreement-scaled uncertainty radius. Selector-Evaluator Value Assessment (SEVA) uses selector critics to order candidates and a separately parameterized evaluator critic to review the selected value, then caps the reviewed value at the selector reference. Dynamic Adaptive Risk-aware Enhancement (DARE) then regulates each residual correction using candidate reliability, the gap between selector and evaluator signals, and a finite stage factor. Together, CARS, SEVA, and DARE form CARE-VI, an evidence-regulated target construction framework that preserves the backbone interfaces for critic regression and actor updates. The analysis bounds the CARS boundary error, the SEVA selected-value overestimation, and the one-sided deviation of the DARE residual displacement from its population counterpart, and establishes fixed-policy recovery after the finite-stage perturbation ends. Experiments with SAC, TD3, and TD7 on four MuJoCo tasks show that CARE-VI achieves the highest mean return in all twelve settings. Grouped ablations and scalar diagnostics support the roles of the three components in improving target reliability.

</details>


### [227] [QoS-Aware Federated Learning for Multimodal In-Cabin Interaction in Smart Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20123)
*Baran Can Gül,Mert Nakıp,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出异步事件触发联邦学习框架FedQoS，通过双阶段门控机制解耦本地计算与全局通信，在保证个性化精度的同时大幅降低QoS违规与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能网联车依赖多模态传感器提供个性化座舱服务，但车载网络QoS具有异构且时变的特性。标准联邦学习的刚性同步轮次无法适应资源不对称，导致安全关键任务的时序违规与能耗枯竭，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的融合训练方案。

Method: FedQoS采用两阶段门控：1）资源感知训练门，仅在传感缓冲与能量储备达到安全阈值时启动本地学习，避免ML任务影响车辆核心移动性；2）QoS感知传输策略，基于兼顾模型新颖度与瞬时延迟、能耗的效率分数决定是否上传更新。客户端本地优化目标包含陈旧度感知的近端项，按更新年龄动态调整全局锚点强度。

Result: 在多模态车载数据集上，FedQoS相比FedAvg仅产生边际性能损失即可获得有竞争力的个性化精度，同时显著减少QoS违规，通信开销降低76.7%，延迟成本降低26.0%。

Conclusion: FedQoS在精度与效率之间实现了高度有利的平衡，适用于真实车载部署场景。

Abstract: Modern smart vehicles leverage multimodal sensors, ranging from high-bandwidth vision systems to low-rate physiological monitors, to provide personalized in-cabin services. However, integrating high-fidelity multimodal fusion with collaborative training is often hindered by the heterogeneous and time-varying Quality of Service (QoS) constraints of vehicular networks. Standard Federated Learning (FL) approaches enforce rigid synchronous rounds that fail to account for these resource asymmetries, leading to safety-critical timing violations and energy exhaustion. In this paper, we propose FedQoS, a novel asynchronous, event-triggered FL framework that decouples local computation from global communication via a two-phase gating mechanism. First, we introduce a resource-aware training gate that initializes local learning only when sensing buffers and energy reserves meet safety thresholds, preventing ML tasks from compromising core vehicle mobility. Second, a QoS-aware transmission policy gates uplink updates based on an efficiency score that balances model novelty against instantaneous latency and energy costs. Locally, clients optimize an objective featuring a staleness-aware proximal term that dynamically adjusts the global anchor strength based on update age. Extensive experiments on multimodal vehicular datasets demonstrate that FedQoS achieves competitive personalized accuracy with only marginal performance loss compared to FedAvg, while substantially reducing QoS violations, cutting communication overhead by 76.7\%, and lowering latency cost by 26.0\%, demonstrating a highly favorable accuracy and efficiency balance for real-world vehicular deployments.

</details>


### [228] [Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20129)
*Xin Chen,Gil Kur,Alexander Shevchenko,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）与线性表示假设（LRH）的局部稀疏异常检测框架，用于大语言模型的部署时安全检测，仅依赖安全数据建模即可标记分布外输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署时安全方法多依赖监督学习并需要不安全训练数据，难以覆盖不断出现的新攻击与危害类别；改用异常检测思路仅建模安全数据，但LLM激活处于高维空间，其统计可行性存疑。

Method: 作者从线性表示假设出发，指出在SAE恢复的概念空间中，邻近点共享一个较小的公共活跃支撑集；基于这一局部稀疏性，提出局部掩码的SAE异常检测框架，并给出理论论证。

Result: 在多种架构与数据集（包括能力测试数据集和安全专用数据集）上验证有效；在允许使用1%分布外数据进行校准的情况下，局部稀疏方法达到接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 局部稀疏方法能够在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元参与计算的情况下，捕获有意义的安信息，说明LLM激活空间中的异常检测在统计上是可行的。

Abstract: Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.

</details>


### [229] [Fast-varying Natural Frequencies and Damping Ratio Identification for Linear Time-Varying System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20138)
*Melisa Bozaci,Alice Cicirello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理增强机器学习方法，将LSTM网络与扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）结合，用于在快速变化的固有频率和阻尼比工况下识别线性时变（LTV）系统。基于2叶片海上风机有限元模型数据验证，FA-1模态固有频率识别的最大RMSE为0.0012 Hz，并可近似识别该模态阻尼比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTV系统在时变工况下（尤其是海上风机受风浪载荷导致固有频率与阻尼比快速变化）的辨识极具挑战。纯粹数据驱动方法缺乏物理约束，而传统辨识方法（如协方差驱动随机子空间辨识）在快速时变条件下精度有限，因此需要融合物理知识与机器学习以提升辨识精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 将LSTM网络与EKF相结合，输入为振动数据（位移与速度测量）、模态阻尼比领域知识以及可给出近似固有频率时变模型的物理模型；用2叶片海上风机有限元模型在真实环境与工况下生成的合成数据验证；评估在阻尼比等系统信息假设不正确时的鲁棒性；并扩展到通过网格搜索、比较基于EKF估计与物理数据训练的模型之间的RMSE来辨识FA-1模态阻尼比。

Result: 可准确识别快速变化的FA-1（1st Fore-Aft）模态固有频率，最大RMSE为0.0012 Hz；基于EKF估计训练的模型依赖准确的阻尼值，而基于物理数据训练的模型对错误的阻尼假设具有鲁棒性；通过网格搜索可较好近似FA-1模态阻尼比，效果优于协方差驱动随机子空间辨识。

Conclusion: 物理增强机器学习方法能有效辨识快速时变工况下LTV系统的固有频率，并在阻尼比辨识上相较传统方法有改进；融入物理信息可增强模型对不准确系统参数的鲁棒性，具备适用于不同环境与运行工况的潜力。

Abstract: This work proposes a physics-enhanced machine learning approach for the system identification of Linear Time-Varying (LTV) systems under time-varying operating conditions in terms of fast-varying natural frequencies and damping ratios by combining a long short-term memory network with an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). The proposed approach uses vibration data (displacement and velocity measurements), domain knowledge of modal damping ratios, and a physics-based model that can yield an approximate natural frequencies time-dependency model. The approach is validated using synthetic data generated from a finite element model of a 2-blade offshore wind turbine under realistic environmental and operating conditions. This system displays fast time-varying frequencies due to operating conditions, whose identification is particularly challenging because of the wind and wave loading. The robustness of the proposed approach is assessed under assumed incorrect system information (e.g. damping ratio). The proposed approach is evaluated across different environmental and operating conditions to show its applicability to different operating regimes. The results show the approach can accurately identify the selected fast-varying natural frequency, 1st Fore-Aft (FA-1) mode, with a maximum root mean square error of 0.0012 Hz. The results demonstrate that the model trained on EKF estimates depends on accurate damping values, whereas the model trained on physics-based data exhibits robustness to incorrect damping assumptions. The approach is extended to damping ratio identification for the selected mode by estimating the root mean square error between models trained on EKF estimates and physics-based data. The results show that the approach can yield a good approximation of the FA-1 mode damping ratio using grid search, offering an improvement over covariance-driven stochastic subspace identification.

</details>


### [230] [QUALS: Corpus Equilibrium for Universal Forecasting via Pattern Quantization and Learnability Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20156)
*Yujie Li,Zezhi Shao,Chengqing Yu,Yisong Fu,Weijie Zhu,Yifan Du,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang,Yongjun Xu,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUALS是一个大规模时间序列语料库均衡框架，通过模式量化与可学习性同步两大机制优化数据采样，显著提升预训练数据效率与零样本预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型研究偏重架构创新，忽视数据多样性，多采用简单采样策略，难以有效处理复杂数据分布，导致训练数据利用效率低、性能次优。

Method: QUALS包含两大核心机制：一是模式量化框架，通过向量量化和均匀分箱系统性地解码混合语料库中的异构模式；二是可学习性同步框架，为异构模式校准采样权重，弥合简单与复杂模体间的优化差距。

Result: 大量基准测试表明，在QUALS上预训练即使大幅缩减训练预算，仍能一致取得更优的零样本预测性能。

Conclusion: QUALS通过语料库均衡显著提升数据效率，使现有模型仅用原训练数据的一小部分即可达到更优性能，为时间序列基础模型预训练提供了高效的数据层面解决方案。

Abstract: Ubiquitous time series data across diverse domains enables critical applications in areas such as transportation systems and power grids. Recently, training foundation models on massive datasets to achieve accurate zero-shot forecasting has emerged as a major research focus. However, current studies predominantly prioritize architectural innovations while insufficiently addressing data diversity, often relying on simple data sampling strategies that fail to manage complex data distributions effectively, leading to inefficient use of training data and suboptimal performance. To address this, we propose QUALS, a large-scale time series corpus equilibrium framework. QUALS significantly enhances data efficiency, i.e., enabling existing models to achieve superior performance using only a small fraction of the original training data. Specifically, QUALS operates through two core mechanisms. First, a pattern quantization framework systematically decodes heterogeneous patterns from mixed corpora via vector quantization and uniform binning. Second, a learnability synchronization framework calibrates sampling weights for heterogeneous patterns, bridging the optimization gap between simple and complex motifs to maximize overall training efficiency. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate that pre-training on QUALS consistently achieves superior zero-shot performance, even under substantially reduced training budgets.

</details>


### [231] [A Noise Optimum in Rehearsal-Free Continual Learning: Isolation, Mechanism, and Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20162)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在纯仿真中研究持续学习里"注入随机噪声可提升旧任务保持率、但过量则下降（倒U形）"这一现象，隔离出产生最优点的关键因素为"锚定增益与噪声方差σ²的耦合"，并界定其适用范围与边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有观察显示向巩固规则注入随机噪声可提高网络对早期任务的保持，但存在一个最优噪声水平，超过后性能下降（保持率与噪声呈倒U形）。作者希望弄清这种最优点的成因机制，并明确该现象成立的条件与边界。

Method: (1) 在多个相关任务持续学习基准上复现该倒U现象；(2) 通过幅度匹配的对比实验隔离出效果需"朝向已巩固权重的相干恢复力"；(3) 将锚定增益与注入噪声方差σ²耦合，并与固定增益（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Adaptation）及后验方差增益（MESU）对比；用受迫Ornstein-Uhlenbeck计算推导上升沿；(4) 通过rotated-vs-permuted对照定位任务结构边界；(5) 考察任务数量对优势的影响及旋转族的紧凑群恒等式。全部为仿真实验。

Result: 倒U现象在Split-MNIST、FashionMNIST、持续Yin-Yang等基准上出现；随机方向、同幅度的力不产生最优点，而朝向错误目标的相干力反而有害；把锚定增益耦合到σ²可恢复大部分最优效果；受迫OU计算预测最优噪声随每任务干扰g上升，方向性在与既有测量对比中获样本外确认（指数在本网格下未定）；倒U在permuted-MNIST上缺失，任务结构是边界条件；匹配严重度下优势随任务数增加而衰减，且无任何旋转族可归因该趋势。

Conclusion: 该倒U最优源于对已巩固权重的相干恢复力与"增益-噪声方差耦合"规则，需共享任务结构才能成立；Doob h-变换的障碍条件化作为低σ安全网限制遗忘；硬件演示另文报告，本文不作硬件声明。

Abstract: Injecting stochastic noise into a consolidation rule can improve a network's retention of earlier tasks up to an optimal level, then degrade it -- an inverted-U in retention vs. noise. This paper isolates what produces that optimum and maps where it holds, entirely in simulation. (1) Phenomenon: the retention inverted-U appears on several related-task continual-learning benchmarks (Split-MNIST, FashionMNIST, continual Yin-Yang). (2) Isolation: a magnitude-matched ladder shows the effect requires coherent restoring toward the consolidated weights -- a random-direction force of identical magnitude produces no optimum, and a coherent force toward the wrong target actively hurts. (3) Active ingredient: most of the optimum is recovered by coupling the anchor gain to the injected-noise variance sigma^2 -- a one-line rule that neither Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Adaptation (fixed gain) nor MESU (posterior-variance gain) implements. A forced Ornstein-Uhlenbeck calculation derives the rising flank and predicts that the optimal noise rises with per-task interference g -- confirmed out-of-sample in direction against pre-existing measurements (the exponent is unresolved at our grid). The barrier-conditioning of the originating Doob h-transform is a low-sigma safety net that bounds forgetting where the coupled gain is too weak. (4) Scope: the optimum requires shared task structure -- it is absent on permuted-MNIST, and a controlled rotated-vs-permuted comparison localizes the boundary to task structure; the precise governing quantity is left open. (5) Length: at matched severity the advantage persists but attenuates with task count, and we show no rotation family can attribute the trend (a compact-group identity). A single-seed BrainScaleS-2 demonstration of the originating rule is reported separately (Howe, arXiv:2607.06924); this paper makes no hardware claim.

</details>


### [232] [Small Enough to Know Everything: The Fully-Enumerable Transformer as an Instrument for the Science of Delayed Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20166)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将"完全可枚举任务上训练的小型Transformer"作为研究grokking的"模式生物"，并通过预注册的跨尺度守恒实验验证了三条任务侧规律的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking研究中，大模型近似设定存在泛化上限无法精确计算、任务变量难以独立操控、无法直接观测全部权重、"不grok"难以量化等缺陷。作者认为完全可枚举任务上的微小Transformer构成一种"科学仪器"，可提供近似设定无法给出的四种能力。

Method: 以完全可枚举任务（每个输入可评估、泛化上限可精确计算、多随机种子成本极低）训练微小Transformer，利用精确可证伪的泛化上限、任务手术（固定其他变量只操控单一结构变量）、全权重直接观测、多种子生存时间统计（将"不grok"视为删失观测）。针对"小参数规律未必外推"的质疑，开展预注册守恒研究：在12K参数建立的恢复性-上限律、角色冲突延迟律、权重衰减响应律，在12K、1M、50M（4000倍跨度，360次运行+44次对照）下用相同从零训练协议重测。

Result: 上限律与延迟律被守恒（0/144 Holm校正上限违反；各尺度Spearman rho≥0.75，置换检验p<1e-4）；权重衰减律系统性形变，随规模增大而变陡。预注册对照显示50M角色冲突缺陷在学习率调整与三倍预算下依然存在。守恒判定标准在数据收集前冻结，且其中一条规律的形变证明该检验本可失败。

Conclusion: 完全可枚举Transformer可作为研究延迟泛化任务侧规律的模式生物：它能精确测量的规律，更大模型大体遵守；而在其偏离之处，偏离本身是有规律、可测量的。

Abstract: Tiny transformers trained on fully-enumerable tasks occupy an unusual position in the study of grokking: every input can be evaluated, every generalization ceiling can be computed exactly, and hundreds of seeds cost minutes. We argue this regime is a scientific instrument with four capabilities that approximate settings cannot offer: (a) exact, falsifiable generalization ceilings; (b) task surgery that manipulates one structural variable while provably fixing all others; (c) direct observation of every weight; and (d) survival-time statistics over many seeds that recast "does not grok" as a censored observation. The obvious objection is that laws characterized at 10^4 parameters may not mean anything beyond them. We answer it with a preregistered conservation study: three task-side laws established at 12K parameters -- a recoverability-ceiling law, a role-conflict delay law, and a weight-decay response law -- are re-measured under an identical from-scratch protocol at 12K, 1M, and 50M parameters (a 4,000x span; 360 runs plus a 44-run control arm). The ceiling law and the delay law are conserved (0/144 Holm-corrected ceiling violations; Spearman rho >= 0.75 at every scale, permutation p < 1e-4), while the weight-decay law deforms systematically, steepening with scale. Preregistered controls show the 50M role-conflict deficit survives learning-rate adjustment and a tripled budget. Conservation was tested against criteria frozen before data collection, and one law's deformation shows the test could have failed. These results license the fully-enumerable transformer as a model organism for the task-side laws of delayed generalization: what it measures exactly, larger models largely obey -- and where they deviate, the deviation is itself lawful and measurable.

</details>


### [233] [Support Thresholds, Not Algorithms, Limit Rare-Association Recovery in Co-Purchase Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20171)
*Xiao Han,Zhen Zhang,Xin Zhao,Jiechun Lei,Moxuan Zheng,Youting Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对市场篮子分析中Apriori支持度阈值的权衡问题，在Instacart与Dunnhumby两个杂货数据集上比较了五种共购边过滤方法，发现top-K与噪声校正（NC）方法在稀有高提升度关联的保留上显著优于Apriori。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apriori算法的支持度阈值存在两难：高阈值只保留高频关联，低阈值则会产生大量规则。因此需要寻找更优的共购边过滤方法，以在保留高频关联的同时有效捕获稀有但高提升度的关联。

Method: 在两个杂货数据集（Instacart约320万篮子、Dunnhumby约20.8万篮子）上比较五种共购边过滤方法：Apriori、Apriori+lift后过滤、基于lift的top-K排序，以及两种网络方法——噪声校正（NC）和视差过滤（DF）。并采用滚动原点留出法进行评价。

Result: top-K方法实现最大平均提升度，NC通过单一显著性参数α达到相近提升度水平。两者对稀有高提升度关联的恢复率显著高于Apriori（80–100% 对 22–28%）。NC与top-K所选边存在18–29%不重叠：NC保留统计验证的关联对，top-K保留高提升度但统计显著性低的稀有对。滚动原点留出评估显示top-K边在各切分点复发率更高，但NC边在留出网络中保持统计显著的可能性约高12个百分点。

Conclusion: top-K与噪声校正（NC）方法在稀有高提升度关联的恢复上明显优于传统Apriori，且二者侧重点不同：NC强调统计显著性，top-K强调提升度。方法选择应依据分析目标在复发稳定性与统计可靠性之间的权衡。

Abstract: The support threshold of the Apriori algorithm involves a trade-off in conducting market basket analysis: the associations that occur frequently are noted with high threshold; however, the low ones lead to generating the large amount of rules. The paper compares five methods for co-purchase edge filtration on two grocery datasets: i.e., Instacart (3.2 million baskets) and Dunnhumby (208 thousand baskets), including Apriori, Apriori + lift post-filtering, top-$K$ ranking based on lift, and two methods based on networks, noise-corrected (NC) and disparity filter (DF). The top-$K$ method ensures the maximum average lift, while the NC achieves similar lift level by means of a single value of the significance parameter ($α$). These two methods recover substantially more rare high-lift associations than Apriori (80-100% against 22-28%). NC and top-$K$ select meaningfully different edges (18-29% non-overlapping): NC retains statistically validated pairs, while top-$K$ retains rare pairs with high lift but low statistical significance. A rolling-origin holdout evaluation shows that top-$K$ edges recur at higher rates at every split, but NC edges are ~12 pp more likely to remain statistically significant in the held-out network.

</details>


### [234] [Robust Federated Q-Learning with Almost No Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20174)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在少量对抗性智能体时联邦强化学习的协作加速问题，提出 Robust Fed-Q 算法，融合基于模型与无模型 RL 思想及中位数均值技术，实现精确收敛、近最优有限时间速率，且通信轮数为 $\tilde{O}(1)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习中多个智能体通过中心服务器协作学习最优价值函数，但少量对抗性智能体可任意行动，破坏协作学习。核心问题是：在此对抗环境下，协作样本复杂度加速是否仍可实现。

Method: 提出 Robust Fed-Q 联邦 Q-learning 算法，结合基于模型与无模型 RL 的思想，并引入稳健统计中的中位数均值（median-of-means）机制以抵御对抗性智能体的污染。

Result: 证明 Robust Fed-Q 在高概率下：(i) 在样本数趋于无穷时保证精确收敛至最优价值函数；(ii) 享有受益于协作的近最优有限时间收敛速率。此外，达成上述保证仅需 $\tilde{O}(1)$ 轮通信，这在以通信为主要瓶颈的联邦学习中具有独立价值。

Conclusion: 即使存在对抗性智能体，联邦强化学习仍可实现协作带来的样本复杂度加速，且通信开销极低，表明稳健性与高效率可兼得。

Abstract: We consider a federated reinforcement learning setting involving $M$ agents, all of whom interact with a common Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents exchange information via a central server to learn the optimal value function. Our goal is to understand to what extent one can hope for collaborative sample-complexity speedups in such a setting, when a small fraction of the agents are adversarial and can act arbitrarily. To that end, we propose Robust Fed-Q}, a federated Q-learning algorithm that blends ideas from both model-based and model-free RL, along with the median-of-means device from robust statistics. We prove that despite corruption, with high-probability, Robust Fed-Q (i) guarantees exact convergence to the optimal value function in the limit of infinite samples, and (ii) enjoys near-optimal finite-time rates that benefit from collaboration. In addition, our approach requires just $\tilde{O}(1)$ rounds of communication to achieve each of the above guarantees, a feature of independent interest in FL where communication is the major bottleneck.

</details>


### [235] [When Does Retrieval Help Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20193)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Elham Buxton,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 检索插件带来的增益并非来自机制本身，而取决于窗口长度 $S$ 与主导季节周期 $L$ 的关系这一运行点；按该关系分层评估可揭示增益所属的体制，并提出两个可解释统计量用于部署前筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍报告检索插件能稳定提升深度预测器性能，并将增益归因于各自的机制设计。但标准评估协议从未变动窗口长度 $S$ 与主导季节周期 $L$ 的关系这一维度，作者因此质疑增益的真正来源，试图检验其是否实际归属于运行点而非插件机制。

Method: 按 $S$ 与 $L$ 的关系对评估进行分层；在 $S{=}12$ 下用"重复上一观测周期"的简单对照与六个标准骨干模型、最强插件对比（7 个基准）；对周期基准测试零样本预训练的基础模型；设计合成扫描实验，控制预测长度、周期与窗口，分析增益边界的相关性；设置无相位可恢复的配对对照检验相位饥饿假设；比较精确查表与图扩散；构造趋势检验与陈旧率两个可解释统计量，在留一数据集评估下预测每单元增益符号。

Result: 在 $S{=}12$ 时，简单对照在 7 个基准中的 4 个上以 8%–44% MSE 优势整体优于六个骨干模型，在 ETTm1 上优于最强插件、在 ECL 上与之持平，但在训练集频谱缺乏集中共享周期的三个数据集上最多差 25%。合成扫描显示增益边界与周期相关（+0.71）而非预测长度（−0.23）；无相位配对对照几乎消除效应，支持相位饥饿解释。零样本基础模型在周期基准上落后训练骨干 22%–50%。精确查表与图扩散效果相当。两统计量达到 0.76 准确率，高于多数规则 0.69 与 22 特征堆叠 0.57。

Conclusion: 检索插件的收益本质上是"查阅记录"的运行点效应，而非插件机制本身；贡献在于提出该体制地图、揭示它的评估协议，以及在部署前进行筛选的两个统计量，而非新插件。

Abstract: Retrieval plug-ins supply a deep forecaster with information its lookback window cannot carry. Published evaluations report consistent gains, and each credits its own mechanism. We show that the benefit belongs instead to the operating point: the relation between window length $S$ and dominant seasonal period $L$, an axis the standard protocol never varies. Stratifying the evaluation by that relation exposes the regime. At $S{=}12$, a simple control that repeats the last observed period beats the six standard backbones, in aggregate, on four of seven benchmarks by $8\%$ to $44\%$ of MSE. It beats the strongest plug-in we run on ETTm1 and matches it on ECL. It is worse by up to $25\%$ on the three datasets whose training-split spectra lack a concentrated, shared period. A controlled synthetic sweep of horizon, period, and window shows the benefit boundary tracks the period (correlation $+0.71$), not the horizon ($-0.23$). A paired control with no phase to recover nearly erases the effect, consistent with phase starvation. Zero-shot pretraining does not escape it: a foundation model trails trained backbones by $22\%$ to $50\%$ on the periodic benchmarks. Within our instrument, exact lookup matches graph diffusion: the payoff is consulting the record, not the machinery on top. Two interpretable statistics, a trend test and a staleness rate, predict the sign of the per-cell benefit at $0.76$ accuracy under leave-one-dataset-out evaluation, a suggestive margin over the $0.69$ majority rule, where a 22-feature stack manages $0.57$. We propose no new plug-in. The contribution is the regime map, the protocol that reveals it, and two statistics that screen it before deployment. Code: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/retrieval-regime.

</details>


### [236] [SoftTri: Smooth Triangular Membership Functions for Adaptive Fuzzy Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20194)
*Babak Sarani,Rahman Ardakanian,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftTri，一种基于soft-hinge机制的可微三角隶属函数，在保持经典三角MF几何结构与局部性的同时实现C∞光滑，并给出解析梯度，用于Takagi-Sugeno模糊神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典三角隶属函数可解释性强、参数少、局部性好，但在节点处不可微，限制了梯度优化在自适应神经模糊架构中的效果，常需次梯度近似或启发式平滑。

Method: 受Swish型激活启发，采用平滑soft-hinge机制构造可微三角隶属函数SoftTri，对输入变量和隶属参数(a,b,c)在任意有限锐度参数β>0下均具C∞光滑性，并推导闭式解析梯度以支持端到端反向传播；将其集成到网格划分规则的Takagi-Sugeno模糊神经网络中。

Result: 在一维、二维非线性逼近基准及Airfoil Self-Noise真实回归任务上，SoftTri相比经典三角MF持续提升优化稳定性与逼近精度，在相同规则结构和训练设置下性能与高斯MF相当或更优。

Conclusion: SoftTri在现代神经模糊学习系统中有效平衡了可解释性与可微优化。

Abstract: Triangular membership functions (MFs) are widely used in fuzzy systems because of their interpretability, low parameterization complexity, and strong locality properties. However, their inherent nondifferentiability at knot points limits the effectiveness of gradient-based optimization in adaptive neuro-fuzzy architectures, often necessitating subgradient approximations or heuristic smoothing techniques. In this paper, we propose \emph{SoftTri}, a differentiable triangular membership function constructed using a smooth soft-hinge mechanism inspired by Swish-type activations. The proposed formulation preserves the geometric structure and localized behavior of classical triangular MFs while providing $C^\infty$ smoothness with respect to both the input variable and the membership parameters $(a,b,c)$ for any finite sharpness parameter $β>0$. Closed-form analytical gradients are derived to enable efficient and fully differentiable backpropagation-based learning. SoftTri is integrated into a Takagi--Sugeno fuzzy neural network with grid-partitioned rules and evaluated on multiple one-dimensional and two-dimensional nonlinear approximation benchmarks as well as a real-world regression task using the Airfoil Self-Noise dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that SoftTri consistently improves optimization stability and approximation accuracy compared with classical triangular membership functions, while achieving performance comparable to or better than Gaussian MFs under identical rule structures and training settings. The proposed approach provides an effective compromise between interpretability and differentiable optimization in modern neuro-fuzzy learning systems.

</details>


### [237] [Evaluating Financial Sentiment in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20198)
*Arslan Bisharat,Oudom Hean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估12种情感模型（词典法、金融专用Transformer、开源LLM），从语言学效度与经济效度两个维度比较，发现通用LLM在无微调下分类表现与金融专用模型相当，但更高分类精度并不带来更强经济关联：多模型情感指标与盈余惊喜显著相关，却均与次日股价收益无显著关系，且模型表现随盈余惊喜幅度增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融情感指标在实证金融中广泛使用，但通用大语言模型（LLM）是否能超越既有金融专用方法尚不明确；同时，分类准确率能否转化为更强的经济关联也缺乏系统检验。

Method: 选取十二种情感模型，涵盖基于词典的方法、金融专用Transformer模型以及开源LLM；采用双重评估标准——语言学效度（分类性能）与经济效度（与盈余惊喜、次日股票收益等经济变量的关联）；并按盈余惊喜幅度（大幅超预期/低于预期 vs. 温和惊喜）分组考察模型表现。

Result: 通用LLM在不进行任务特定微调的情况下，分类性能与金融专用Transformer模型相当；但更高的分类准确率并未转化为更强的经济关系。若干模型生成的情感指标与盈余惊喜显著相关，但没有一个模型的情感指标与次日股票收益显著相关。模型表现在盈余大幅超预期或大幅低于预期的公告中最强，在盈余惊喜较温和的公告中明显减弱。

Conclusion: 金融情感能够捕捉公司经济业绩的相关信息，但解释短期市场反应的能力有限；分类精度与经济效度之间存在脱节，提示评估金融情感模型时不应仅依赖语言学指标。

Abstract: Financial sentiment measures are widely used in empirical finance, but it remains unclear whether general-purpose large language models (LLMs) improve on existing finance-specific methods. This paper evaluates twelve sentiment models, including dictionary-based methods, finance-specific transformers, and open-source LLMs, using two criteria: linguistic validity and economic validity. We find that general-purpose LLMs achieve classification performance comparable to finance-specific transformer models without task-specific fine-tuning. However, higher classification accuracy does not translate into stronger economic relationships. Several models produce sentiment measures that are significantly associated with earnings surprises, but none is significantly associated with next-day stock returns. Model performance is strongest for announcements with large earnings beats or misses and substantially weaker for announcements with more moderate earnings surprises. These findings suggest that financial sentiment captures information about firms' economic performance but has limited ability to explain short-run market reactions

</details>


### [238] [Towards a Unified Modality-Agnostic Multimodal Framework for Cognitive Workload Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20199)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Calvin Joseph,Giorgos Giannakakis,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个统一的、模态无关的层级式Transformer架构，用于在单一模型中处理异构生物信号，并通过试点研究评估五种模态的所有31种组合在认知负荷测量中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知负荷对自适应人机系统设计至关重要，生物信号测量已有大量研究，但关于异构生物信号模态组合效果的研究仍然有限，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 开发统一的、模态无关的层级式Transformer架构，在单模型内处理异构生物信号；对ECG、EDA、RESP、SpO₂、EEG五种模态的全部31种组合进行试点评估，采用留一被试交叉验证，跨三种认知任务（抽象推理IQ、算术解题MATH、游戏GAME）测试。

Result: (i) EEG是最强单模态，在IQ、GAME及合并的ALL设置中排名最高；(ii) 增加模态并不稳定提升性能；(iii) 五模态全组合在IQ上取得最高平均分73.02%，在IQ、MATH、GAME、ALL四个评估设置上平均后为68.08%；(iv) 相比晚融合方案，所提方法模型规模减少约50%，同时保持更低的推理时间。

Conclusion: 异构生物信号模态的简单叠加并不必然带来性能提升；统一层级式Transformer架构能在保持较低推理时间的同时显著减小模型规模，为认知负荷评估的多模态融合提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cognitive workload reflects the mental effort required during task performance and is central to the design of adaptive human-machine systems. The use of biosignals to measure cognitive workload has been extensively researched and documented; however, studies examining the effects of combining heterogeneous biosignal modalities for this purpose remain limited. To provide insight into this area, we developed a unified, modality-agnostic, hierarchical Transformer-based architecture to process heterogeneous biosignal modalities within a single model. We use this framework in a pilot study evaluating all $31$ possible combinations of five modalities: Electrocardiogram (ECG), Electrodermal Activity (EDA), Respiration (RESP), Peripheral Oxygen Saturation (SpO$_2$), and Electroencephalogram (EEG), under leave-one-subject-out validation across three cognitively distinct tasks: abstract reasoning (IQ), arithmetic problem solving (MATH), and a game task (GAME). In this pilot setting, the results suggest that: (i) EEG is the strongest single modality, ranking highest in IQ, GAME, and the pooled ALL setting, where samples from all three tasks are combined; (ii) adding more modalities does not consistently improve performance; (iii) the full five-modality combination achieves the highest \textit{Average} score of $73.02%$ on IQ and $68.08%$ when the \textit{Average} scores are averaged over the four evaluation settings: IQ, MATH, GAME, and ALL; and (iv) the proposed method reduces model size by approximately $50%$ compared with late-fusion alternatives while maintaining a lower inference time.

</details>


### [239] [Scene-Conditioned Relation Routing for urban cellular activity forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20209)
*Qingzhong Li,Jingye Lin,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出场景条件空间关系路由框架SCRR-Net，将城市上下文用于同时控制空间依赖选择与跨任务知识迁移，在米兰和特伦托数据集上于短信、网络流量与通话活动预测任务中持续优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市蜂窝活动预测需联合建模短信、移动网络流量与通话活动等异构时空信号。现有方法将时间建模、空间关系学习与多信号预测分开处理，依赖固定图结构或静态多任务学习方案，难以适应不断变化的城市场景。

Method: 提出SCRR-Net，一种场景条件的空间关系路由框架，由城市上下文信息联合控制空间依赖选择与跨任务知识迁移。模型包含四个组件：上下文编码器、空间图专家路由模块、时间Transformer编码器、任务知识路由模块。

Result: 在Milano与Trento数据集上的实验表明，SCRR-Net在短信、网络流量和通话活动预测上一致优于竞争方法，并具有可解释的路由行为。

Conclusion: 以场景条件驱动的路由机制统一空间关系学习与跨任务知识迁移，可提升城市蜂窝活动多信号预测的适应性与精度，并兼具可解释性。

Abstract: Urban cellular activity forecasting requires jointly modeling heterogeneous spatiotemporal signals, including SMS usage, mobile network traffic, and call activity. Existing methods often separate temporal modeling, spatial relation learning, and multi-signal prediction, relying on fixed graph structures or static multi-task learning schemes, which limits their adaptability to changing urban scenes. We propose SCRR-Net, a scene-conditioned spatial relation routing framework in which urban contextual information jointly controls spatial dependency selection and cross-task knowledge transfer. SCRR-Net includes a context encoder, a spatial graph expert routing module, a temporal Transformer encoder, and a task knowledge routing module. Experiments on the Milano and Trento datasets demonstrate that SCRR-Net consistently outperforms competing methods on SMS, network traffic, and call activity forecasting, while providing interpretable routing behaviors.

</details>


### [240] [Subdomain-aware representation compression for pretrained image embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20213)
*Poowanut Niamluang,Jittat Fakcharoenphol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将降维技术（PCA、LDA）从全局数据集应用拓展到子域表示压缩，在图像域上验证其在空间与计算复杂度上的优势，并意外发现精度优于直接使用完整嵌入，同时展示了压缩表示的迁移学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维通常作为提升空间效率的技术，被均匀地应用于整个数据集。作者希望探究将降维用于"子域（subdomain）表示压缩"的可行性，即针对数据子域而非整体数据做压缩，以服务于边缘设备机器学习对空间与计算复杂度的严苛需求。

Method: 在图像域中探索并应用标准降维技术，主要包括主成分分析（PCA）与线性判别分析（LDA），对子域表示进行压缩；并与直接使用完整嵌入（full-embedding）的流程进行对比实验，此外还设计了实验来验证基于压缩表示的迁移学习能力。

Result: 结果不仅证实了在空间和计算复杂度上的预期改进——这对边缘设备ML应用至关重要——还显示在精度上相较直接完整嵌入流程有所提升。作者给出的一种解释是：降维有效地抽取了子域特征。迁移学习实验进一步表明压缩表示具备可迁移性。

Conclusion: 降维可有效用于子域表示压缩，在降低空间与计算开销的同时提升精度，并支持迁移学习；其效果可能源于降维对子域特征的抽取作用，适合边缘设备场景。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is a well-known technique for improving space efficiency, typically applied uniformly across an entire dataset. This paper investigates the possibilities of using dimensionality reduction techniques for subdomain representation compression. We explore standard techniques such as Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear discriminant analysis (LDA) in image domains. The results not only demonstrate the expected improvements in space and computation complexity crucial for edge-device ML applications but also show improvements in accuracy over direct full-embedding procedure. One possible explanation is that dimensionality reduction effectively extracts subdomain features. We also performed experiments to demonstrate transfer learning capabilities using the compressed representations.

</details>


### [241] [Explaining spatial information flow in short-term traffic forecasting models using a gated graph attention network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20217)
*Yue Li,Shujuan Chen,Ying Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在GAT层引入可学习门控，量化邻居信息占比，实现对ST-MetaNet空间依赖的渐进式消融与解释，发现两层GAT高度冗余、门控轻微正则可小幅提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAT层通常被描述为捕捉邻近位置的影响，但其注意力权重无法与任何实测物理量对比，因而缺乏验证；由此遗留两个问题：模型应如何解释，以及哪些组件是必要的。

Method: 在GAT层中加入门控，在每个传感器和每个时间步学习"传感器更新状态中来自邻居而非自身的比例"；对门控施加正则化可逐步撤回邻居信息，形成分级的消融手段。将门控GAT应用于ST-MetaNet（编码器与解码器各在两层循环层之间放置一个GAT层），用英格兰战略路网498个环形检测器一整年的记录训练。

Result: 门控为重载交通的传感器分配更大的邻居信息占比，并遵循出行量的日、周周期，与"繁忙时相邻位置耦合更强"一致；轻度正则化略微提升精度，正则化过强则因撤回模型所需信息而使精度下降。编码器门控先于解码器门控关闭，但直接消融修正了这一顺序：单独移除任一GAT层精度至少与保留两层相当，同时移除两层则精度显著下降，说明两层大体冗余而非任一层不可或缺。

Conclusion: 门控GAT带来适度的精度提升，提供了空间信息在何处、何时流动的解释，并给出了架构需要哪些层的证据。

Abstract: Short-term traffic forecasting supports real-time monitoring and control of road networks, and graph attention networks (GAT) are the standard means of representing spatial dependence in these models. GAT layers are widely described as capturing the influence of neighbouring locations, but this is seldom verified, because the attention weights offered in support cannot be compared against any measured quantity. That leaves two questions open, how the model should be explained and which of its components are necessary. We address this by adding a gate to the GAT layer which learns, at every sensor and every time step, what share of a sensor's updated state is drawn from its neighbours rather than from itself. Regularising the gate withdraws neighbour information progressively and thereby provides a graded form of ablation. We apply the gated GAT to ST-MetaNet, whose encoder and decoder each place one GAT layer between two recurrent layers, and train it on one calendar year of records from 498 loop detectors on the strategic road network of England. The gate assigns a larger share of neighbour information to sensors carrying heavier traffic and follows the daily and weekly cycle of travel, consistent with adjacent locations being more strongly coupled when busy. Mild regularisation improves accuracy slightly, and accuracy declines at higher strengths as the penalty withdraws information the model needs. The encoder gate closes before the decoder gate, but direct ablation qualifies that ordering. Removing either GAT layer alone leaves accuracy at least as good as keeping both, whereas removing both degrades it substantially, so the two layers are largely redundant rather than either being indispensable. The gated GAT therefore yields a modest accuracy gain, an explanation of where and when spatial information flows, and evidence on which layers the architecture requires.

</details>


### [242] [Is It Still Worth Training a Classical Model in the Era of LLMs? A Crossover Benchmark on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20218)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究量化了"提示冻结LLM"与"训练经典模型"在表格预测任务上的数据交叉点N*：在18个表格数据集、126次独立评估中，训练模型所需标注量通常极少即可超过小规模冻结LLM，中位交叉点约为训练集的6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流表格工具（如Excel中的Copilot、Claude for Excel）已支持无需训练的LLM行级标注，但许多商业预测问题中标注成本高昂，实践者需要明确：应直接提示冻结LLM，还是采集数据训练模型，以及需要多少标注数据。该问题此前缺乏系统的定量回答。

Method: 提出"有标注数据交叉点N*"指标，即训练模型的学习曲线追平冻结LLM恒定（平坦）误差时的训练集规模。汇总18个表格数据集上、8种提示配置下的126次独立学生评估（小规模GPT模型），并与六类经典模型族的幂律学习曲线配对；同时检验少样本示例的缩放规律与协议可复现性，并做特征名语义的受控探测。

Result: 训练胜出很快：即便给LLM最优提示配置，训练经典模型在86%的情况下用现有标注量即可胜出；在40%情况下用评测中最小的标注子集即可胜出；观测交叉点中位数为训练集的约6%。上下文少样本示例不呈现训练式幂律缩放规律；由独立实施者复跑同一协议，变异系数为0.148。受控探测显示LLM依赖可识别的特征名语义，说明该交叉点可能是保守估计（作者未声称记忆效应）。

Conclusion: 对典型商业表格问题，证据明确支持：采集几百条标注并训练梯度提升模型，比提示冻结LLM更经济有效。

Abstract: Large language models can label a tabular row from a plain-English description with no training - a capability now shipping in mainstream spreadsheet tools such as Microsoft Copilot in Excel and Anthropic's Claude for Excel - raising a practical question for the many business prediction problems where labels are expensive: should you prompt a frozen LLM, or collect data and train a model - and if so, how much data? We quantify the answer with the labeled-data crossover N*, the training-set size at which a trained classical model's learning curve overtakes a frozen LLM's training-free (and therefore flat) error. Aggregating 126 independent student evaluations of small GPT models under eight prompting configurations across 18 tabular datasets, paired with authoritative power-law learning curves for six classical model families, we find that training wins fast: even given an oracle choice of its best prompt configuration, a trained classical model beats the small frozen LLM using no more labeled data than is already on hand in 86% of cases, and wins by the smallest labeled subset we evaluate in 40%, with the observed crossover at a median of ~6% of the training set. In-context few-shot examples do not behave like training - error versus shot count does not follow a power law - and the same protocol re-run by independent implementers varies with a coefficient of variation of 0.148. A controlled probe indicates the LLM depends on recognizable feature-name semantics, which plausibly makes our crossover a conservative estimate (we do not claim memorization). For a typical business table, the evidence is clear: collect a few hundred labels and train a gradient-boosted model.

</details>


### [243] [Accuracy Is Not Enough: A Cross-Architecture Audit of Demographic Bias in Deep Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20249)
